# Changelog

All notable changes to Hydra are documented here. Every PR (human-, Claude-, or
Hydra-authored) MUST add an entry under **Unreleased** — see AGENTS.md
"Documentation & Changelog Discipline". Format follows
[Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/); newest first within a section.



## [Unreleased]

### Fixed
- Fixed: worktree reconciler no longer removes live worktrees during long LLM turns — the 30-minute idle floor was raised to 6 hours (single-lane providers make workers think 30m+ between writes; 69 `[Registry] FATAL: worktree` worker deaths on 2026-10-29). Registry-keyed liveness fix is beaded separately.

### Added
- Added: RED contract tests for the Node dependency preflight close gate (`internal/core/gate_deps_preflight_test.go` + inert stub `internal/core/gate_deps_preflight.go`, bead hydra-8vds) — pins the FR-001-companion contract: a `package.json` with no `node_modules` runs the project install in the worktree before the verification command, no manifest skips the install, an install that dirties tracked files fails the tree-clean guard (verification command never runs), and the verification output (--- PASS lines and all) is forwarded verbatim. Tests skip loudly pre-GREEN; `GATE_DEPS_ENFORCE=1` fails them while the contract is unimplemented. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: RED contract tests for the prd-lint coverage-threshold semantics rule (`tools/prd-lint/main_test.go`, hydra-74mf) — pins that an Acceptance Criterion naming a coverage threshold over a file glob (e.g. `coverage >= 80% for glob tools/**`) without an explicit exemption marker yields a `coverage-threshold` warning pointing at the owning block with a line number and message, and that coverage-free PRDs lint clean (the marker suppresses the warning). Tests skip loudly pre-GREEN and fail under `PRDLINT_COVERAGE_THRESHOLD_ENFORCE=1` (enforced run currently RED: rule not implemented). *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: campaign-wide park via label sweep (`internal/core/campaign_park.go`, `dag_navigator.go`, `scheduler.go`, `overseer.go`) — when a campaign is parked, `sweepCampaignParkLabels` applies the `park-gate` label to all non-terminal members (skipping already-gated and non-members; `campaign_park_leak` event on in_progress members, deduped by a once-per-bead ledger) and when unparked removes the label from every non-terminal member; `DAGNavigator.GetReadyBeads` excludes park-gated beads, and the sweep is wired fail-open at every `campaignParked` consult point in scheduler/overseer. GREEN for the hydra-xchh RED contract tests (9/9 PASS). *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: dependent-release rollback on rejected close (`internal/core/build_dependency_graph.go`, `internal/core/worker_lifecycle.go`) — `DependencyGraph.RollbackReleases`/`DAG.RollbackReleases` re-block dependents released by a completion (predicate `InDegree==0 && StatusOpen`, mirroring `MarkCompleted` release semantics, so rollback is idempotent), and `RollbackReleasedDependents` wraps it fail-open on graph errors, emitting exactly one `release_rolled_back` event (`reason=close_rejected`) with the re-blocked count when a close is rejected and dependents were released. GREEN for the hydra-63bh RED contract tests (7/7 PASS). *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: GREEN for per-bead evidence and evidence-based chain checks (FR-003, validator-truth PRD) — `internal/validation/bead_evidence.go` now implements `BeadEvidence` (one `git log --all` sweep + one batched `diff-tree --stdin` per call regardless of matching-commit count, whole-word bead-id matching, `includeDirty` dirty set via `status -uall` excluding `.beads/`, `ls-tree` blob ids, deterministic sha256 `path:blob-id` fingerprint, fail-open on non-repo/git faults) and `ValidateTDDChainEvidence` (RED needs a test file with a meaningful assertion, GREEN needs a non-docs non-stub implementation file, declared-files-no-evidence gaps name the bead id, unlocated/empty sides stay vacuous) reusing `beadfiles.Resolve`/`IsTestFile` and `DetectStubs` — no second parser. `TDDValidator.hasMeaningfulAssertions` upgraded to case-sensitive assertion patterns (`func Test`, `t.Run(`, `assert.`, `def test_`, …) so comments and bare `import "testing"` no longer read as tests; `beadEvidenceImplemented=true` enables the previously-skipping contract tests. The original strand failed on a provider-credit 402 before any code landed; this pass landed GREEN on top of the RED chain. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: RED contract suite for per-bead evidence and evidence-based chain checks (FR-003, validator-truth PRD) — `internal/validation/bead_evidence_test.go` pins `BeadEvidence` (whole-word bead-id commit grep, dirty set only with `includeDirty`, non-repo fail-open, deterministic sha256 fingerprint) and `internal/validation/tdd_validator_test.go` pins `ValidateTDDChainEvidence` (RED needs a test file with a case-sensitive meaningful assertion, GREEN needs a non-docs non-stub implementation file, declared-files-no-evidence gaps, unlocated sides produce no gaps). Inert stubs in `bead_evidence.go` keep the package compiling; tests skip loudly pre-GREEN and fail under `BEAD_EVIDENCE_ENFORCE=1`. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*

### Fixed
- Fixed: inverted condition in `TestCampaignPark_GateBlockedBeadNotReady` (`internal/core/campaign_park_test.go`) — the label-removal fallback ran only when `AddNode` succeeded, but `AddNode` rejects duplicates, so the DAG node kept the `park-gate` label and the readiness-restore half of the test could never pass (daemon gate FAIL, hydra-5m1a gate log). Condition now matches the test's own comment ("AddNode rejects duplicates; update through the node instead"); implementation untouched. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: `processHygiene.logRpcSilent` nil-map panic — hygiene is constructed at four sites and only `ensureHygiene` initialized `rpcSilentLogged`, so an rpc-active/token-silent worker crashed the daemon (4 restarts 2026-10-29). Lazy-init under the existing mutex makes every construction site safe.
- Fixed: infra classification at the executable-acceptance gate (`internal/core/gate_infra_class.go`, `internal/core/bead_closer_hard_gates.go`) — a verification command failing with exit 127 (command not found, e.g. `sh: 1: jest: not found`) or a signal death now reopens the bead as `infra-failure` WITHOUT consuming a verification strike, tracked by a durable `infra-reject-N` label; the 4th consecutive infra rejection escalates with reason `infra-stuck` (`escalated` + `needs-human` + `infra-stuck` labels, one `bead.escalated` event), and a passing gate clears the counter. Project failures (exit 1/2/3/...) keep the existing `verification-failed` strike path, so environment churn (missing tooling, provider kills) no longer burns `validation.max_rounds`. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: dep-open close rejections no longer take a verification strike (`internal/core/bead_closer_hard_gates.go`) — a chain member closed before its dependency is reopened and requeued, bounded by a durable `dep-open-requeue-N` label; the fifth consecutive rejection escalates (`escalated` + `needs-human` + `verified-close-escalated`), and a passing dependency gate clears the counter, so ordering races no longer exhaust `validation.max_rounds`. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: store-ownership preflight precision (`internal/core/spawn_preflight.go`) — the spawn preflight warning now names up to 3 actual offending paths in the store (e.g. `.beads/.br_history: uid 2008`) instead of a generic store-wide string, and when a `br list --json --limit 1` probe exits 0 it logs at debug level only (no `spawn_warn_store_ownership` event); a failing probe keeps warn + event.
- Fixed: flaky `TestWorkerCompletion_ConcurrentCompletions` and 5 sibling worker-completion tests (`internal/core/worker_completion_test.go`) — the test helper now disables Graphify in its config (the async graphify indexer raced the TempDir RemoveAll cleanup: `unlinkat ...: directory not empty`) and writes deterministic close-evidence files (`close-evidence.txt` in each fixture repo) instead of relying on the race's side effect (graphify's async write previously supplied the verified-close evidence gate's changed file). Test-only change; production Graphify default untouched. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*

### Changed
- Refactor campaign-park label sweep (`internal/core/campaign_park.go`) — the duplicated parked/unpark branches inside `sweepCampaignParkLabels` extracted into per-bead handlers `campaignParkGateBead` (idempotent attach + once-per-bead `campaign_park_leak` note) and `campaignParkUngateBead` (strip), with one shared member-scan loop selecting the handler. Label names, event payloads, error semantics, and counts byte-identical; `TestCampaignPark_` 9/9 PASS before and after, `go vet`/`gofmt` clean. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Adopt the shared target-file resolver in the close path (FR-006, close-path-truth PRD; `internal/core/beadfiles_adoption.go`, `bead_closer.go`) — `beadTargetFiles` now delegates to `internal/beadfiles.Resolve` (single parser for validators and the close path, so the two file lists can no longer drift; `:symbol` suffixes and `TBD` normalized away) plus one-directional sibling-test expansion: every resolved impl file `x.go` gains `x_test.go` in scope, so a RED bead whose worker wrote only the sibling test file no longer rejects as `empty-diff`; a bead declaring only test files gains no impl scope. One `TestBeadfilesAdoption_*` regression test per behavior-changed caller (scope gate `hasTargetChanges`, prespawn probe, target overlap, satisfied close, fix audit, integration retest, daemon close gates); the close-path suite, affected caller suites, `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/core/` all green; new adoption code at 100% statement coverage. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Refactor close-gate rejection counters (`internal/core/gate_infra_class.go`, `internal/core/bead_closer_hard_gates.go`) — the duplicated infra (`infraRejectCount`/`infraLabels`/`clearInfraRejectLabels`) and dep-open (`depOpenRequeueCount`/`depOpenLabels`/`clearDepOpenRequeueLabels`) helper trios unify into prefix-parameterized `maxLabelCount`/`currentLabels`/`clearCounterLabels`; event payloads, label names, and escalation semantics unchanged. `TestGateInfra_` 5/5 PASS, close-gate regression set PASS, `go vet` clean. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Refactor dependent-release rollback (`internal/core/build_dependency_graph.go`, `internal/core/worker_lifecycle.go`, `internal/core/dag_rollback_test.go`) — the "released by this completion" predicate extracted into `releasedByCompletion`, the rollback event constants and nil-safe `emitReleaseRolledBack` helper replace inline emission in `RollbackReleasedDependents`, the DAG rollback no longer pre-allocates an empty slice, and the test readiness/blocked helpers dedupe into `dagRollbackBeadInList`. Behavior identical; `TestDagRollback_` 5/5 PASS before and after. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Refactor `internal/beadfiles/resolve.go`: precedence keys, test-file suffixes and files-line prefixes hoisted to named package vars; `## Context` json-block scan split out of `contextJSONFiles` into `contextJSONBlock`. Behavior unchanged; no new parser.

No code change. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Recorded the SYNTAX verification round for dependent-release rollback on rejected close (bead hydra-y9mr): `go vet ./...`, `go build ./...` and `go test ./internal/core/ -run 'TestDagRollback_' -count=1 -v` (5/5 `--- PASS`) all clean; gofmt clean on the three target files, zero placeholder comments, doc comments present on `RollbackReleasedDependents`, `RollbackReleases` and the refactor helpers. No code change. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*

### Added
- RED: campaign-wide park label-sweep contract tests (`internal/core/campaign_park_test.go` + inert stub `internal/core/campaign_park.go`) pin the park-gate semantics — a pre-existing `park-gate` label on an UNRELATED bead must not block the campaign's members (hydra-gcip leak fixture), membership is `bead.Project == key` only so a campaign-2 bead depending on a campaign-1 member is never swept, closing the PARK-GATE removes member labels and restores DAG readiness, an in-progress member emits exactly one `campaign_park_leak` event per project across repeated sweeps, and a store listing error fails open with zero label writes. Behavioral tests SKIP loudly pre-GREEN; `CAMPAIGN_PARK_ENFORCE=1` fails them (proves RED). Predicates `campaignParkGateActive`/`campaignParkMember` are live and 100% covered. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- RED: dependent-release rollback contract tests (`internal/core/dag_rollback_test.go`) pin the rejected-close rollback semantics — worker completion marks B completed and releases dependent D (baseline, passes today); rejection of a bead with no released dependents emits no `release_rolled_back` event; rollback is idempotent (second pass re-blocks nothing and emits nothing new); a verified close keeps dependents released. Inert stubs (`RollbackReleasedDependents` in worker_lifecycle.go, `RollbackReleases` on DependencyGraph, `dagRollbackImplemented=false`) keep the default suite green; `DAG_ROLLBACK_ENFORCE=1` fails the four contract tests loudly for the GREEN phase. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added `internal/beadfiles` shared target-file resolver package (`Resolve`, `SplitFileList`): extracts a bead's declared target files with the same precedence as `internal/core`'s `beadTargetFiles` — metadata `files` over `target_files`, then the first `Files:` description line, preserving the `TBD` skip and comma/semicolon/space/tab separators. No adoption yet; a later bead swaps `internal/core` call sites to this package so the scope gate, spawn scope check and close-path diff scoping cannot drift.
- FR-5 RED contract suite for the mirror-health SLO/docs deliverable (`internal/core/mirror_health_docs_test.go`, 10 tests gated by `MIRROR_HEALTH_DOCS_ENFORCE`): pins `bash -n scripts/mirror-health-slo.sh` + exit 0 on a healthy fixture / 1 on drift or missing clean coverage / 2 on missing tools, the read-only store contract, `docs/architecture/shepherd.md` naming every FR-2 knob (`mirror_health.enabled` / `audit_interval` / guarded `auto_resync` OFF-by-default), the AGENTS.md operating-knobs row, and the post-repair runbook line `br sync --import-only`. Tests SKIP loudly pre-GREEN; `MIRROR_HEALTH_DOCS_ENFORCE=1` runs RED and currently fails the shepherd/AGENTS/runbook tests (missing doc content) plus the SLO infra test (`"date: command not found"` → exit 127 instead of 2 — GREEN must add the required-tools probe).
- Added guarded auto-resync defaults for mirror health: config loader now runs `restoreMirrorHealthDefaults` on the Overseer section at both parsing sites in `internal/core/config.go` (ensures the newly introduced `mirror_health` knobs not present in a user's yaml fall back to their zero-value-off defaults instead of being lost during `raw -> config` copying).
- Added `BrUpdater` seam in `internal/core/overseer.go` (`BeadsUpdater` alias plus `beadsBridgeUpdater` adapting `BeadsClient`) so the mirror-health heal primitive (`br update <id> --status <same>`) routes through the same br CLI path used by every other caller; the new `mirror_health.auto_resync` knob gates guarded healing, defaulting to false (audit-only drift detection).

### Fixed
- Raised `internal/mirroraudit` coverage from 90.8% to 96.9%: production comparer shell-out path now covered by `TestComparerBrListJSON_AllPaths` (binary-lookup failure, command success with dir/args asserted, command failure) via the `execLookPath`/`execCommand` test seams.
- `internal/core` compiled again: `pan_mirror_health.go` had a syntax error (unclosed `Event{...}` call) plus undefined symbols (`mirroraudit.DefaultComparer`, `mirroraudit.Diverness`, `now`) that failed the lint gate for every bead in the package. `mirrorGuardHooks` now lives in production code and the sweep body stays gated off (`mirrorResyncImplemented=false`) until the mirror-health chain's RED sweep tests are made consistent. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- FR-4 Pan filing RED contract tests landed (internal/core/pan_mirror_health_test.go, 2 tests): a user-owned divergent store files exactly one pan bead (filing sink invoked once, one `mirror_drift_needs_human` event, never two filings for one record) and a filing-sink failure is fail-open (attempt logged, zero events, sweep continues and retries on the next tick after the sink heals). Tests are compiling against the current RED stub via the `mirrorWriteProbe` seam (inert, `nil` = probe succeeds) and the `mockBeadsClient.createErr` injection surface; they skip loudly until `mirrorResyncImplemented` flips, and `MIRROR_HEALTH_ENFORCE=1 go test ./internal/core/ -run 'TestMirrorHealthFiling'` proves RED detection (`got 0 calls: []` on both). Narrow suite `go test ./internal/core/ -run 'MirrorHealth' -count=1` exits 0. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- FR-3 guarded auto-resync RED contract tests landed (`internal/core/pan_mirror_health_test.go`, 6 tests): db_ahead terminal record heals via a stubbed `BrUpdater` once `mirror_health.auto_resync` is true, non-terminal jsonl records never heal, user-owned divergent stores and write-probe failures never heal (`mirrorWriteProbe` inert seam), and the knob defaults to OFF (audit-only). All tests skip loudly until `mirrorResyncImplemented` flips; `MIRROR_HEALTH_ENFORCE=1` proves RED detection. Narrow suite: `go test ./internal/core/ -run 'MirrorHealth' -count=1` PASS. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- `internal/mirroraudit/audit.go` gofmt drift (struct tag alignment in `Report`) — lint gate violation introduced by the FR-2 shared-package refactor. No behavior change; `go test ./internal/mirroraudit/ -count=1` still green with 85.5% coverage.
- Resolved `go build ./...` compile gate fault in `internal/core/pan_mirror_health.go` — the healing operation referred to an undefined `o.brUpdater` field; the call is now routed through the existing `o.beadsClient.UpdateBeadStatus(...)` wrapper to restore compileability.
- Fixed `br list --json` shape handling: newer `br` versions return an `{"issues":[...],"total":N}` envelope instead of a bare array; `mirroraudit.parseBrList` now accepts both. The audit comparers (shared package and `tools/beads-mirror-audit`) also pass `--all --limit 0` so closed beads are included — without it live stores reported false drift (49 closed beads classified `jsonl_ahead`).

### Documentation
- PRD contract compliance gate run for the fleet-loop-degradation PRD (hydra-qumh): all six checks pass; placeholder/phantom tool raw failures adjudicated (classifier literals + pre-existing-at-base files), PRD file-name drift dispositioned — full report in docs/hydra/handoff/task-qumh.md.
- FR-4 discovery for Pan filing on non-healable drift: contract decisions pinned in docs/discovery/mirror-health-fr4-discovery.md (filing sink = beadsClient.CreateBead, exactly-once per record via the pan_stale_filing ledger pattern, fail-open events, FR-3 guard classification consumed from the shared decision point), with the write-probe seam landed on the heal path. RED contract tests + notes in internal/core/pan_mirror_health_test.go (FR-4 block) and the handoff in docs/hydra/handoff/discovery-wyel.md.
- RED pin for the single-source-of-truth audit contract: `internal/mirroraudit/single_source_test.go` (untracked until GREEN) pins `tools/beads-mirror-audit` to import the shared `internal/mirroraudit` package and delete its duplicate parser/classifier. Evidence + contract in docs/discovery/mirror-audit-single-source-red.md.

### Added
- Daemon mirror-health sweep (`internal/core/pan_mirror_health.go`) now runs through the shared `internal/mirroraudit` package — periodic read-only audit of beads jsonl vs br DB with quiescence/live-worker guards, typed events (`mirror_drift_detected`/`mirror_audit_clean`/`mirror_audit_error`), and ledger at `$HYDRA_HOME/mirror-health-ledger.json`. Wired the sweep into `Overseer.tick()` via new `mirror_health.enabled` / `mirror_health.audit_interval` knobs in `OverseerConfig` (default true / 30m). Added a single-source-of-truth regression test (`internal/mirroraudit/single_source_test.go`) pinning the CLI audit tool to the shared package so classification can never drift. Verified: `go test ./internal/mirroraudit/`, `go build ./...`, and live audit on `srv-jobfile` store (49 beads, mirror clean). *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*

### Documentation
- Discovery: FR-002 periodic read-only mirror audit sweep (docs/discovery/mirror-health-fr2-discovery.md) — design modeled on `internal/core/pan_stale_filing.go`, config knobs `mirror_health.enabled` / `mirror_health.audit_interval` (default 30m, 0 disables), guards (jsonl mtime < 2m quiescence skip, live-worker skip), ledger at `$HYDRA_HOME/mirror-health-ledger.json`, typed events `mirror_drift_detected` / `mirror_audit_clean` / `mirror_audit_error`, AC mapping + RED test plan. No code change (discovery bead).
- Added mirror-health SLO check (`scripts/mirror-health-slo.sh`) and shepherd docs — operator now has a one-command post-repair verification for beads jsonl/db drift (0 `mirror_drift_detected` and ≥1 `mirror_audit_clean` per store in the last 24h), documented in `docs/architecture/shepherd.md` §1b.
- PRD-4 `docs/PRDs/mirror-health-prd-20261022.md`: beads jsonl/db drift audit sweep + guarded healing (audit-only default). Follows 2026-10-22 manual fix of `ajantha-srv-jobfile-7db` (stale mirror since Oct 5 direct-sqlite surgery) and fleet audit that surfaced 10 terminal drift records.
- Fixed: host pi runtime migrated from npm-global to a managed installation (`/opt/pi-managed`, 755, world-executable) — npm installs don't pin transitive deps and pi warned on every update. `/usr/bin/pi` re-pointed at the managed launcher; hindsight-pi extension in the shared config pinned to `fix/hindsight-0.10-bank-profile` @ `fdc9347` (hindsight-api 0.10.x removed bank-profile endpoints → extension showed "offline"); 65/65 extension tests + live smoke green. Docs: host-setup.md §5 rewritten + hindsight pin note. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Refactor: token-budget pack digest trim (bead hydra-btt6) — `ApplyPackTokenBudget` in `internal/core/context_pack.go` now tracks the pack's marshaled char count incrementally (subtracting each elided digest's JSON length + one comma) instead of re-marshaling the whole pack per trim (O(N) → O(1) per elision); `packTokenEstimate` renamed to `packCharLength` (returns raw chars; the chars/4 token estimate happens in the budget loop). Behavior identical: oldest-first trimming, current bead never elided, `elided: id1, id2, ...` footer; `go test ./internal/core/ -run 'ContextPack' -count=1` PASS. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Documentation: round-1 validation of the deterministic payload drift-check (bead test-xq3e, verify-first close) — the TDD gap "RED bead hydra-b2em has no test files (target_files metadata missing)" is stale: `internal/core/bead_claims_test.go` and the landed implementation already exist and `go test ./internal/core/ -run 'BeadClaims|DriftCheck' -count=1` passes. No code change; handoff at docs/hydra/handoff/test-xq3e.md. Fix, if the gate persists, is bead metadata (br update target_files). *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Documentation: validation of FR-004 bounded close-hold (bead test-qcuz, verify-first close; prior round test-g7a9, verify-first close) — the earlier "Test Results: PASS: 0 passed" was a wrapper artifact of a non-verbose run; `go test ./cmd/worker/ -run 'CloseHold' -count=1 -v` proves all 7 CloseHold tests pass (hold below cap, forced at cap, nil-verdict fail-open, label count, event shape). No code change; handoff at docs/hydra/handoff/test-g7a9.md. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Refactor: deterministic payload drift-check (`internal/core/bead_claims.go`) decomposed — `matchClaimLine(line) (BeadClaim, bool)` extracted from `parseBeadClaims`, probe-outcome switch in `checkBeadClaims` replaced with `classifyClaimStatus(output, err)` + named status constants (`claimStatusPass/Fail/Unknown`). Per-claim body extracted into `evaluateClaim(beadID, claim) bool` (hard-fail returns false, all else true — fail-closed gate made explicit); redundant Go-1.25 loop shadow removed. Behavior identical; `go test ./internal/core/ -run 'BeadClaims|DriftCheck' -count=1` PASS. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: GREEN: deterministic payload drift-check at spawn preflight. `internal/core/bead_claims.go` (`BeadClaim`, `parseBeadClaims`, `claimVerifyTimeout` 2s, `beadClaimChecker`) and `internal/core/spawn_preflight.go` (`claimDriftCheckV2`) now parse `claim: <assertion> (verify: <cmd>)` lines from a bead's description/payload and verify them at spawn preflight with a 2s timeout. Results split: `pass`, `fail`, `unknown` (timeout or parse error). `hard:`-prefixed claims fail spawn (fail-closed) with a `claim_hard_failed` event; soft claims warn with `claim_soft_failed` and spawn proceeds. Zero claims → no-op and no events (backward compatible). *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
### Changed
- Refactored `tools/beads-mirror-audit/main.go`: converted the divergence lookup from a nested O(N·M) loop to an id→status map (O(N+M)), extracted the duplicated `<id> jsonl=<s> db=<s>` line formatting into `divergenceLine`, simplified `main`'s output branching, and added doc comments on all exported symbols. Behavior identical; `go test ./tools/beads-mirror-audit/ -count=1` PASS, `gofmt`/`go vet` clean.
### Fixed
- Fixed `scripts/mirror-health-slo.sh` YAML parsing — the registered-store regex/sed only matched list-dash-prefixed `path:` keys, silently returning 0 stores for the standard `hydra.yaml` shape (`- name: x\n    path: y`); matcher now accepts both `- path:`/indented `path:` and the SLO script was end-to-end verified against a mock store set (SLO-OK rc=0). *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: Resource monitoring now reads `/proc` natively instead of forking `/bin/sh`/`/bin/bash` — `getProcessCount` walks `/proc` for numeric PIDs and `getProcessLimit` parses `/proc/self/limits` directly, eliminating the fork failure mode (EAGAIN / `fork: retry: Resource temporarily unavailable`) that wedges the daemon exactly when the process ceiling is hit, the very condition the monitor guards against. Behavior identical otherwise. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Green close-out re-verification done for task-tu42, docs accuracy ensured.
### Added
- Added: FR-006 same-fingerprint verification classifier. `internal/core/bead_closer.go` now stores CloseGate rejection fingerprints as durable `gate-fp-<first8>` labels on the bead; when the identical fingerprint (sha256 of bead_id + verification command + classification reason) repeats, one typed `verification_substantive` event is emitted instead of re-labelling. `internal/core/bead_closer_fingerprint_test.go` (6 contract tests) pins label shape, repeat-dedup, per-fingerprint isolation, and determinism. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- GREEN: tools/beads-mirror-audit implemented — `--store <dir>` parses `<dir>/issues.jsonl` (JSON or `id status` token lines) against a single `br list --json` call, prints `<id> jsonl=<status> db=<status>` per divergence (missing-from-db counts as drift, db-only beads skipped), `mirror clean (N beads)` on agreement, `--json` emits `{divergences,checked}`, exit 0/1/2 with `audit unavailable: <err>` fail-open reporting; read-only. Plus in-process test file (main_inproc_test.go) driving the `brRunner` seam for 68.1% statement coverage.
- GREEN: Stale-filing sweep runs on overseer tick and auto-closes `pan-filed` beads whose spawn-wedge/cohort premise has demonstrably cleared (filing older than 48h, cohort empty, spawn healthy in last 24h) — at most one per project per tick with `pan_filing_stale_closed` event; branch-protection reasons stay human-triage-only.
- Refactor: Stale-filing sweep decomposition — extracted the stale-filing candidate filter into `staleFilingCandidates` (typed `staleFilingCandidate` struct, probe order documented, per-cohort probe cache so a store read happens at most once per distinct cohort per sweep) and removed the redundant duplicate `staleFilingLastSpawnOK` probe evaluation (candidates are non-empty only when the spawn-health probe passed) plus a duplicated rate-limit comment in `sweepStalePanFilingsForProject`. Behavior identical; `go build`, `go vet` clean, `go test ./internal/core/ -run 'StaleFiling|PanHealth' -count=1` PASS.
- Discovery: FR-002 Beads mirror audit design doc (docs/discovery/beads-mirror-audit-discovery-s8f0.md) — resolves jsonl-vs-br ambiguity (br authoritative; missing/malformed records count as drift; `br list --json` single-call over per-bead `br show` to dodge WAL-poison windows; exit-2 precedence over parse), maps all 6 ACs against the existing RED stub.
- tools/beads-mirror-audit: RED-phase inert stub + contract tests pinning the bead-mirror audit contract (divergence lines `<id> jsonl=<s> db=<s>`, exit 0/1/2, `--json` report, fake-`br` double, read-only guarantee); behavior tests skip loudly pre-GREEN, `BEADS_MIRROR_AUDIT_ENFORCE=1` fails them to prove RED.
- Added: RED-phase contract tests for the stale-filing sweep covering in-progress filings with cleared premises. `internal/core/pan_stale_filing.go` (inert RED stub: `staleFilingReasonCleared`, `staleFilingMinAge` 48h, `staleFilingSpawnWindow` 24h, `branchProtectionReasonRe`, probe hooks `staleFilingIsOld`/`staleFilingCohortNamed`/`staleFilingCohortBlocked`/`staleFilingLastSpawnOK`, inert `sweepStalePanFilings`, `staleFilingSweepImplemented=false`) + `internal/core/pan_stale_filing_test.go` (6 tests: eligible in_progress + open filings close with the exact cleared reason, branch-protection reason never auto-closed, cohort-still-blocked, no-recent-spawn, young-filing guardrails, rate limit + typed `pan_filing_stale_closed` event shape). Default run skips loudly and exits 0 (`STALE_FILING_ENFORCE=1` fails the 2 contract tests, proving RED detection). *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: RED-phase contract tests for the deterministic payload drift-check at spawn preflight (bead hydra-b2em). `internal/core/bead_claims.go` (inert RED stub: `BeadClaim`, `parseBeadClaims`, `claimVerifyTimeout` 2s, `beadClaimChecker`) + `internal/core/bead_claims_test.go` (7 parse + 7 check subtests). Default run skips loudly and exits 0 (gate stays green pre-implementation); `BEAD_CLAIMS_ENFORCE=1 go test ./internal/core/ -run 'BeadClaims|DriftCheck' -count=1` fails 6/14, proving the RED phase detects the missing contract. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: Added test coverage for `closeWithHold` in `cmd/worker/close_hold_test.go` to exercise the fall-through path when `HYDRA_DAEMON_GATES` is enabled and done is true, as well as the held path. Coverage for `closeWithHold` improved from 0% to 100%.
- Added: rpc-mode token reporting closes the zero-token blind spot (FR-003): the rpc scanner now forwards parsed per-message usage totals to the registry reporter on EVERY usage-bearing event (`turn_end`/`message_end` as well as the default case — exit-only forwarding left mid-session heartbeats at tokens_so_far=0 and Charon's zero-token stall probe flagged actively-streaming workers for `kill-stalled-worker`, PID 2353988 class); `WorkerReport`/`WorkerRecord` carry a `last_rpc_activity` timestamp stamped on every usage and extension-notification event, persisted in the registry, and `detectZeroTokenStalls` (5m `rpcActivityWindow`) requires no rpc activity in the window before flagging — an active-but-token-silent worker logs `[Charon] rpc-active but token-silent: <bead>` once and is excluded from kill-stalled-worker surfacing; the hung-worker heartbeat catch-all (shouldKillAsStuck) is unchanged. Zero-usage blocks never fabricate tokens. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Refactor: FR-004 close-hold dedupe — new `closeWithHold(bead, projectPath, repoName, reason, verdict)` (cmd/worker/close_hold.go) becomes the single close-with-hold entrypoint; both worker close sites (success path + auto-close guard, cmd/worker/main.go) collapse from duplicated `closeHoldFor && runCloseHold` branches into one call. Behavior identical (held = bead stays open; forced at hold_count==2 closes anyway; nil/malformed verdict fails open). `go build ./...` PASS, `go vet` clean, `go test ./cmd/worker/ -run 'CloseHold' -count=1` PASS. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Changed: gofmt normalization pass on lingering unformatted files in `cmd/worker/` (`chain_handoff_test.go`, `close_hold.go`, `execution_report_test.go`, `main_test.go`, `turn_economy_test.go`) and `internal/core/` (`gate_runner_test.go`, `quarantine_reconciler.go`, `worker_completion_test.go`); behavior identical, `go build`/`go vet`/narrow `Token|ZeroToken|Stall` suite re-verified green.
- Changed: zero-token stall hygiene dedupe — `internal/core/process_hygiene.go` extracts the triplicated lazy `j.hygiene` init into `ensureHygiene()` (the `KillStalledWorker` copy had drifted, omitting the `rpcSilentLogged` map) and the once-only `[Charon] rpc-active but token-silent` log into `processHygiene.logRpcSilent`; the stall loop now computes each worker's age once instead of re-deriving `now.Sub(r.StartedAt)` three times. Behavior identical.
- Fixed: FR-006 fingerprint source pinned to the classification string — `verificationFingerprintSource` (internal/core/bead_closer.go) now always hashes `sha256(bead_id + verification command + classification reason)` (e.g. `verification-command-failed`) and deliberately ignores the literal first stderr line, so same-classification failures with different output collide and repeat as a typed `verification_substantive` event instead of fragmenting into distinct `gate-fp-<first8>` labels. Test `TestVerificationFingerprintSource_IgnoresStderr` pins the contract. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Refactor: ContextPack budget-trim loop efficiency and naming cleanup (internal/core/context_pack.go) — `packTokenEstimate` renamed to `packCharLength` (it returns the marshaled char length, from which tokens = chars/4 is derived), and `ApplyPackTokenBudget` now tracks the marshaled size incrementally (subtracting each elided digest's JSON length + one comma) instead of re-marshaling the whole pack on every trim, turning trim cost from O(M×N) to O(N) total; elision order (oldest-first), current-digest retention, footer format, and default-budget semantics are unchanged. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Refactor: Store-ownership preflight precision cleanup — `offendingStorePathsDetail` (internal/core/spawn_preflight.go) now takes the daemon uid as an explicit parameter (no duplicated `daemonUID()` seam call) and its entry loop separates the stat/ownership check from the record-append step; `storeOwnershipDetail(projectPath, storeUID, daemon)` threads the daemon uid through and is the single detail builder called by `spawnPreflightStoreOwnership`. Behavior identical; `StoreOwnership` tests pass. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Changed: Same-fingerprint verification classifier refactor — `trackVerificationFingerprint` (internal/core/bead_closer.go) now uses the existing `hasBeadLabel` helper instead of a hand-rolled label loop, and the rejection-path source selection (first stderr line vs classification reason) is extracted into `verificationFingerprintSource`. Behavior identical. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: `trackVerificationFingerprint` coverage — new `internal/core/bead_closer_fingerprint_test.go` pins the CloseGate rejection-fingerprint contract end-to-end (5 tests): first occurrence stores exactly the durable `gate-fp-<first8>` label and emits nothing; a repeat on the same bead emits one typed `verification_substantive` event carrying `{bead, fingerprint, count:2}` without duplicating the label; a *different* fingerprint on the same bead gets its own label and stays silent (repeat detection is per-fingerprint, not per-bead); a nil beadsClient is a no-op returning nil (no panic); `getVerificationFingerprint` itself is deterministic, 8 hex chars, and differs when any of bead_id / verification command / reason differs.
- Added: Token-budgeted pack digests (FR-007): new `context_pack.max_pack_tokens` config field (int, default 40000) caps ContextPack size; `ApplyPackTokenBudget` (internal/core/worker_spawner.go) trims member digests oldest-first until the pack's estimated token size (chars/4) fits the budget, never eliding the current bead's own digest; on trim it emits a typed `pack_trimmed` event with `{bead, elided_count, budget}` and the pack footer lists skipped bead IDs as `elided: id1, id2, ...`. Tests in `internal/core/context_pack_token_budget_test.go` verify budget enforcement, `pack_trimmed` emission, current-digest retention, and that 120 one-line digests fit the default budget with zero elisions. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: rpc-mode token reporting closes the zero-token blind spot (FR-003): the rpc scanner now forwards parsed per-message usage totals to the registry reporter on EVERY usage-bearing event (`turn_end`/`message_end` as well as the default case — exit-only forwarding left mid-session heartbeats at tokens_so_far=0 and Charon's zero-token stall probe flagged actively-streaming workers for `kill-stalled-worker`, PID 2353988 class); `WorkerReport`/`WorkerRecord` carry a `last_rpc_activity` timestamp stamped on every usage and extension-notification event, persisted in the registry, and `detectZeroTokenStalls` (5m `rpcActivityWindow`) requires no rpc activity in the window before flagging — an active-but-token-silent worker logs `[Charon] rpc-active but token-silent: <bead>` once and is excluded from kill-stalled-worker surfacing; the hung-worker heartbeat catch-all (shouldKillAsStuck) is unchanged. Zero-usage blocks never fabricate tokens. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: FR-001 ContextPack chain-scan terminal exclusion verified complete (discovery phase): `ensureContextPack` counts only non-terminal chain members against the `max_chain_members` cap (default 50, `ContextPackConfig.MaxChainMembers`), terminal fix-beads cost a fetch but never cap pressure or digest/term slots; `internal/core/context_pack_chain_scan_test.go` pins the behavior (6 tests) and `TestContextPack_*` passes 8/8. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: FR-006 same-fingerprint verification classifier (discovery phase): `getVerificationFingerprint` in `internal/core/bead_closer.go` computes `sha256(bead_id + verification command + reason)` (truncated to 8 hex chars) on each CloseGate rejection and stores it via the `gate-fp-<first8>` label; a repeated fingerprint on the same bead emits a typed `verification_substantive` event. Pan sweep prompt gains the sentence that beads carrying `verification_substantive` must NOT receive `repair:clear-escalation`. Implementation is verified complete with tests passing; documented for the next phase.
- Fixed: Store-ownership preflight precision (FR-005): preflight warning now names actual offending paths (up to 3, e.g. `.beads/.br_history: uid 2008`) instead of a generic store-wide string, and a `br list --json --limit 1` probe in the project cwd downgrades the cross-uid warning to debug-only (no `spawn_warn_store_ownership` event) when the daemon can actually read the foreign store. Duplicate/conflicting `offendingStorePathsDetail` definitions resolved to the single implementation reporting real uids. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: bead-closure rejection fingerprints no longer collide on the classification string — `gate_close.go` now passes the gate's raw `Summary` output as the stderr source to `rejectClose`, so `verificationFingerprintSource` hashes the literal first stderr line (instead of falling back to the generic `gate-<name>-failed` / `gate-coverage-truth-failed` reason) when the same classification fails with different output. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: Pan-filed promotion path closes the self-healing loop (2026-10-21 spawn-freeze root cause): a Pan file-bead proposal whose text carries the `code change suggested` marker is now filed as a normal task bead WITHOUT the pan-filed label (filing-time routing in `filePanBead`, cap still applies — proposal filings count toward it), and a new `PanFiledPromoter` reconciler (5m tick, wired in overseer + service_starter) retroactively strips `pan-filed` from non-terminal task beads whose text carries the marker, emitting typed `pan_filed_promoted` events — the fleet can now execute its own code-change diagnoses instead of wedging in front of them; human-triage filings (no marker) stay excluded from discovery. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: Silent container-type skip in the retry spawn loop (scheduler.go) now logs `Skipping container-type bead in retry path` like its sibling skips — the 2026-10-21 05:37–05:52 UTC freeze (28 br-ready → filters → 3 spawnable → nothing) was undiagnosable because that one `continue` emitted nothing. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Added: Process-hygiene sensors (2026-10-20 zero-token stall + rogue-pi incident): Charon now detects workers whose session never completed a turn (state=working, tokens_so_far=0, age>15m — fresh heartbeats alone made the fleet stall invisible) and scans /proc for rogue bare-pi processes outside hydra.service, untracked, older than 2h (live fixture: PID 3002553, root session scope, burning a core through every daemon restart). Both surface as `zero_token_stall_detected`/`rogue_pi_detected` events and Pan digest host anomalies, with two new whitelisted repairs `kill-stalled-worker` (explicit PID only — never by PGID, gates share the daemon process group; marks the row dead and reopens the bead) and `kill-rogue-pi` (explicit PID). killProcess now uses direct syscalls (SIGTERM→SIGKILL) instead of kill(1) so kill failures carry the real reason (syscall.EPERM) — a root-owned rogue the daemon uid cannot signal fails closed with a typed `rogue_pi_kill_denied` escalation naming the root remedy instead of a silent "exit status 1". *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: Worktree mis-rooting + verified-close fail-open cascade (hydra-akn8): `ExistingWorktree` now validates the worktree's common gitdir against the project repo (submodule-aware, `isOurWorktree`) and refuses mis-rooted paths with a typed `worktree_refused/misrooted_gitdir` event; `ensureWorktreeLocked` asserts identity immediately after `worktree add` and removes+fails-closed a mis-rooted creation; verified-close diff/work evidence reads are FAIL-CLOSED (`collectCloseEvidence`/`unionMainCheckoutLeaks` return errors, close rejected with `cannot-verify-diff` and reopened) instead of logging "not blocking" and letting nil changed files pass `hasTargetChanges` as "unknown, fail open" — which let hydra-xnk7/r2rl/htq0 close-verified with zero work (2026-10-20, dubious-ownership exit 128 on a monorepo-rooted chain worktree). Gate-round-2 refinement: `evidenceFatalErr` classifier scopes fail-closed to real-repo git faults (dubious ownership, permission denied); a non-git evidence surface ("not a git repository", missing dir) stays legacy fail-open — git cannot distinguish an unreadable .git from a non-repo, and path-resolution fixtures close against non-existent project paths. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: PushPendingEntry (overseer) now tracks consecutive publish-failure state — Failures count and LastClass (ClassifyPushFailure result) are recorded per project so backoff/escalation logic can distinguish transient vs permanent failure classes. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- Fixed: IntegrationRetest `findBranch` falls back to the bead's chain-root worktree key (`hydra/wt-<chainKey>` / `hydra/quarantine/<chainKey>`), so chain-member beads parked on their root's branch are no longer invisible to the retest sweep. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
- **FR-004 bounded close-hold on self-reported `done:false`** (`hydra-8uw4`): the worker no longer closes a bead as completed when the agent's own HYDRA_VERDICT says `done:false` with a summary — an honest unmet claim must not be promoted to terminal state. Instead the worker emits a typed `worker_close_held` event, persists the hold count as a `close-hold-N` label (replacing the previous one), and leaves the bead open for the retry loop. The hold is bounded at `maxCloseHolds` (2): at the cap the worker closes anyway (`forced: true` in the event) so a permanently failing bead cannot wedge the pipeline. Malformed verdict JSON and `done:false` without a summary fail open — the close proceeds exactly as today. Covered by `cmd/worker/close_hold_test.go` (unit tests plus a real-`br` label persistence test). *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*
### Documentation
- **Stale-filing sweep discovery** (FR-001): synthesized the design context for a closure-side pan-filing sweep into `docs/discovery/stale-filing-sweep-context.md` — inventories the existing premise gate (`verifyPanFilingPremise`), `PrunableWedgeSweep`, and `IntegrationExhaustedSweeper`, names the gap (in-progress filings whose premise already cleared hold a cap slot), and lists six design constraints for the implementation phase (fail-open, no self-firing, reuse premise probes verbatim, respect live-worker filings, typed `pan_filing_closed_stale` events, dedupe/cap interplay).
- **AGENTS.md Discovery Protocol** (`hydra-fva4`): added an explicit `DISCOVERY PROTOCOL (At Bead Start)` step to the Hydra Agent Workflow — run `hindsight_search` once per bead with the bead's key terms, skipping silently when the integration path is already healthy. Makes durable-memory recall a mandatory first step instead of an ad-hoc habit.
- **Gate-Doctor: pi-based triage of verification RED** (`hydra-fva4`): on verification-command RED, a cheap LLM pass (`internal/core/gate_doctor.go`) classifies the failure family (environment / code-broken / stale-gate / flaky / uncertain) and routes the close rejection to the matching bounded action — allowed repair rerun, fix-bead filing with the exact RED output, or escalation. Bounded: the doctor never edits gates/tests and is terminal per bead per attempt. Subsumes the hydra-4nwg fix-bead branch.
- Orphan-claim P0 triage (docs/discovery/orphanclaim-triage-yzi1.md): all three digest claims verified stale at 2026-10-06 14:02 UTC — 0 true orphan claims (4 live workers, escalated/pan-filed excluded by design, rest registry-completed churn reopened by Charon), publisher wedge absent (PR #268 merged, 0 unlanded commits), disk 39%. No code change.

### Added
- **Token-budgeted context-pack digests** (`hydra-8uw4` GREEN): context packs now carry one one-line digest per chain member (`member_digests`), so every spawned worker sees the chain's shape without re-fetching beads. When the pack's estimated size (chars/4) exceeds `context_pack.max_pack_tokens` (default 40000), digests are elided oldest-first — the current bead's own digest is never elided — a `footer` field (`elided: id1, id2, ...`) records what was cut, and exactly one typed `pack_trimmed` event reports `elided_count` and `budget`. Applied on both `pack_created` and `pack_refreshed` write paths.
- **task-breakdown `--validate`: analyze-only compilation pre-check** — `node cli.mjs <prd> --validate` runs the deterministic analyzer and creates nothing, failing loudly (exit 1) on both PRD authoring failure modes seen live (2026-10-18, srv-data-harbour): zero `### FR-*`/`### NFR-*` headers (task-breakdown previously reported `success: true` with an empty skeleton chain) and target-file overlap between FRs (DRYAD single-owner rule). The create-prd skill's Phase 4 now mandates this gate before reporting a saved PRD, including the terminal-FR consolidation pattern for shared config files. Authored during a manual ops session; `Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.`-adjacent context: no worker involved.
- **AIMD governor memory-throttle feedback** (`hydra-ytlh`): memory.high reclaims WITHOUT killing, so during the 2026-10-15 throttle regime (slice pinned at memory.high 16G, 25.4G swapped) the governor — keyed only on OOM/outcome signals — saw a healthy fleet and redlined at max_workers. Each scheduler tick now probes the daemon cgroup's PSI `memory.pressure` avg10 and `memory.current` vs `memory.high`; at/over `overseer.concurrency_governor.memory_pressure_threshold` (default 50%) the limit halves and recovery is re-armed. Fail-open on unreadable cgroups; negative threshold disables.
- **Quarantine reconciler: self-healing chain verdicts** (`hydra-lqce`): one stale `closed-failed` label used to quarantine an entire completed chain forever with no automated appeal (2026-10-16: NFR-001's stale label quarantined the 132-bead srv-smart-engine campaign AFTER PRD validation passed). The reconciler re-verdicts recorded quarantines every 5 minutes; when no member carries `closed-failed` it re-integrates the quarantine branch via the normal IntegrateBranch path and removes it. Tainted members get a bounded revalidation: their declared verification command is rerun in a scratch checkout of the quarantine branch tip (the chain worktree is gone by then and the main checkout lacks the code), and ONLY a green run scrubs the label — escalation/human labels are never touched, red or unrunnable leaves the verdict (fail-closed). Registry at `$HYDRA_HOME/quarantined-chains.json`; legacy quarantines bootstrap from `hydra/quarantine/*` branch scans with membership reconstructed via the registrar's dependents walk.
- **Close-time verdict coherence** (`hydra-lqce` M3): a `completed` close now scrubs a pre-existing `closed-failed` label on the same bead (contradictory state previously allowed) and records `closed-failed-revalidated` + a `label_revalidated` event for audit.

### Fixed
- **ProjectResolver.ResolveRepoPath returned the raw relative segment for multi-repo repos** ("backend" instead of `<project>/backend`): every multi-repo evidence read chdir'd into a nonexistent relative dir — masked for months by the fail-open close, exposed by the fail-closed hardening (2026-10-20 worker_completion multi-repo fixture). Now joins the project root (absolute segments pass through).
- **closeIntegrationBase resolved the multi-repo integration base from the project parent** — which has no git at all (fms doctrine, nipuna.j 2026-09-30) — making every multi-repo close die at "integration base unresolved". The base now resolves from the repo directory; worktree-mode keeps resolving from the project root (worktree HEAD is the chain branch, not the base).
- **Verified-close is fail-closed on evidence-read errors** (`hydra-akn8`): a git error during the diff/work check used to be logged "not blocking" and the close accepted — the mechanism behind 2026-10-20's zero-work "verified" closes (xnk7/r2rl/htq0 closed with a single workers.json in a mis-rooted worktree). Evidence errors now reject the close. Companion fixes: worktree creation/reuse asserts the resolved gitdir belongs to the project repo (submodule-aware), and the legacy worker-completion fixtures now run against real committed git repos instead of the fake `/test/test-project` path (14+ tests that passed vacuously under fail-open).
- **Integration-retest fetch fail-open to local `origin/<base>`** (#312): the daemon uid holds no GitHub credential for user-owned repos, so every retest attempt died at `git fetch origin <base>` with exit 128 (GitHub's auth-masquerade) and burned the hourly cooldown while nothing landed — the parked srv-data-harbour set spun on exactly this after the chain-aware selector fix. Fetch is now best-effort freshness: on failure the stage proceeds with the local tracking ref (verified present in normal operation); a genuinely missing ref still skips the tick.
- **Integration-retest selector-drop observability** (#311): a labeled bead rejected by `selectBead` used to vanish silently (excluded label / active worker / rate-limit / no-branch each returned without a trace), which hid the park-vs-spawn namespace bug for an hour. Each drop now logs its reason (deduped 1/bead/10m), including the resolved chain key on branch-miss.
- **Retest attempt-verification** (`hydra-1hbo`): Pan's blocked-reason for `integration-pending` beads now carries the retest stage's REAL attempt counters (`RetestStats`) — attempts/last/max — and a deterministic `RETEST SILENCE` escalation when a bead sits parked past 90 min with ZERO recorded attempts; the retest stage's silent no-branch skip now logs loudly (once/bead/hour). Ends the 2026-10-19 failure where Pan narrated "retries mechanically" for 70+ minutes while the selector never ran (11 beads stranded).
- **Operator-API park namespace** (`hydra-6tfb`): `POST /projects/:name/park` now resolves the config project name to the scheduler's `projectKey` namespace before writing — parks stored under the raw name were silently ignored by `campaignParked` (a parked campaign kept spawning 10+ min with the API answering 200 parked:true, 2026-10-19).
- **Verification-command gate extracts the fenced command, not prose** — `verificationCommandFor` matched the first "Test Command:" line, and when task-breakdown put a prose instruction line (e.g. "- Run the exact test command: ...") *before* the fenced `**Verification Command:**` block, it passed the prose itself to `sh -c`, which died `Illegal option` exit 2 in ~4ms without running any tests — burning a reject attempt on every such bead (live 2026-10-15: vv3 reject attempt 1). The extractor now looks ahead past the marker line for a fenced ```sh block and takes its command; extractions that read as prose (dash-led bullets or sentence connectives like "the/and/with/should") are rejected outright. Unit-tested against the real task-breakdown description shape (`internal/core/verification_command_test.go`).
- **Gate process hygiene: gates run in their own POSIX process group and are group-killed** — `RunGate`/`RunCoverageTruthGate` previously spawned `sh -c` in the DAEMON's process group (a `kill -PGID` aimed at a stuck gate would have hit the daemon itself) and on timeout/completion killed only the shell while jest/pytest grandchildren held the log pipe and reparented to PID 1 (observed: jest alive 16+ min after the gate artifact recorded exit 2). Gates now `Setpgid` into their own group, a watcher SIGKILLs the whole group on timeout, and a post-`Wait` group kill reaps grandchildren that outlived the shell.
- **Worker-death process leaks**: the Pi child now launches with `PR_SET_PDEATHSIG` (Linux) so a dead worker's Pi is kernel-killed instead of reparenting to PID 1, and Charon's sweep now SIGKILLs the process group of any `.hydra-worktrees/`-referencing process whose parent is PID 1 (covers node/jest grandchildren below Pi; observed live: 20+ reparented node processes, ~9G RSS). The daemon's own group is never swept; a `ps` failure fails open.
's chain worktree, not the main checkout** — pre-integration the main checkout has neither the bead's code nor a fresh dependency install, so a declared `test_command` was RED no matter what the worker did (live 2026-10-15: empty main-checkout `node_modules` → "Module ts-jest ... not found" burned 5 attempts on a syntax bead and escalated a GREEN bead 3/3 on an unwinnable gate). Falls back to main checkout only when no worktree exists; the weekly postclose sweep still re-verifies integrated state on main.
- **Epic auto-close never persisted for user-namespaced bead IDs** (`hydra-ysaz`): `findProjectPathForBead` matched raw `project.Name` prefixes, but bead IDs are user-namespaced (`ajantha-srv-smart-engine-1u3`), so every epic auto-close decision was discarded with `Could not find project path for epic` (185x in one campaign) and epics stayed open forever. Resolution now prefers the DAG bead's `Project` field, with a longest-first namespace-tail fallback. Also fixed the epic close guard being vacuous: it scanned `ListBeads` output for `Dependencies`, which that parser never populates — the designed fail-closed gate was fail-open since it shipped. It now reads the epic's `dependent_ids` from `br show` metadata and verifies each dependent against the session-completed map, the DAG, then the store.

### Fixed
- **Store-ownership spawn preflight downgraded to fail-open** (#296 follow-up): the uid-mismatch heuristic blocked every spawn into user-owned repos (`spawn_blocked_store_ownership` events, zero log lines, silent wedge) even though cross-uid reads work and workers run as the repo owner via `su`. The sidecar uid-lock is a write hazard; the preflight now warns (typed event `spawn_warn_store_ownership` + log) and lets real write failures surface at the write site.
### Added
- **MemAvailable spawn floor** (`hydra-3ocq`, memory-governor follow-up): worker spawn is refused while host MemAvailable is below `resource_monitoring.min_memory_available_mb` (default 2500 = 2.5G), even when the percentage gate passes — the 2026-10-15 OOM kills outran a percentage-only gate because percentage ignores free headroom. Probe errors fail open. Complements the landed per-worker cgroup `memory.max` containment.
- **Per-worker memory containment** (`hydra-3ocq`): each worker (plus pi/jest/npm children) is confined to its own cgroup with `memory.max` (`overseer.worker_memory_max_mb`, default 5120), so a runaway jest heap kills one retryable worker instead of OOM-killing the daemon + entire fleet (4 daemon OOM deaths on 2026-10-15). V8 heaps capped via `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size` (`overseer.worker_node_heap_mb`, default 3072, only when NODE_OPTIONS unset). Fail-open: without cgroup delegation workers run uncapped with a warning. Requires `Delegate=yes` on hydra.service — see docs/operations/host-setup.md.
- **Hard-park reconciler** (stranded worktree work, `hydra/wt-hydra-u5e`): beads labelled `worktree-unavailable` now get probed on a slow loop and auto-unparked after 2 consecutive successful probes (hysteresis), so a transient worktree failure no longer needs a human to clear the label.
- **Non-executable bead types filtered from the retry queue** (residual graft of the Sep-20 escalated-discovery fix): approval-gate/approval/epic beads are stripped from retry eligibility case-insensitively and their retry state is cleared — they can no longer be resurrected from the DAG into a worker spawn.
- **Bead store corruption guard** (srv `.beads` emptied under concurrent access): store-wipe detection and fail-closed exclusion.

### Added
- **Spawn stale-claim regression probes** (`hydra-ebjq`): 2 tests pinning that a transient spawn failure after the in-memory spawning placeholder claims the slot leaves zero `activeWorkers` entries and the bead is immediately re-spawnable, and that a phantom claim with no tracker row self-heals on the next spawn attempt (verify-first close: the reported 45-min silent-skip wedge is not reproducible on this base — rollback defer, `reap-placeholder` repair, spawn-failure TTL purge and Charon pool-deadlock reap all exist and are tested).
- **PR-wave 2 (integration/pr-wave2-20261013)**: 10 more stranded fleet PRs landed — token-usage rollup job (spend trends survive registry pruning, #104), Pan filed-bead cap as config knob with cap-hit escalation (#105), PR aging sweep with weekly per-owner digest (#111), git-access/ownership spawn preflight with remediation text (#190), sensor symmetry (fire+resolve typed events, #193), DAG edge integrity pruning (#189), worktree graphify-out symlink (#282), single-bead synthesis + dangling-work detector refactors (#154, #211), create-prd execution-type producer contract docs (#135). Shape-classification (#115) and close-time hard gates (#204) were already superseded by development; their stale rounds were verified subset and dropped. Operators: token spend now persists across registry prunes; git-ownership wedges fail closed at spawn with the exact `safe.directory` fix; aging human-merge PRs surface in a weekly digest instead of piling silently.

### Added
- RED-phase test suite `internal/core/bead_closer_hard_gates_test.go`: pins the three FR-002 close-time hard gates (executable acceptance with verbatim-output rejection, quarantine contradiction, dependency-order warn-only singletons) plus unchanged VerifiedClose round/escalation semantics. All 10 tests fail at RED with `runCloseHardGates undefined` — the GREEN bead implements the gate entry point.
- GREEN: `BeadCloser.runCloseHardGates` (internal/core/bead_closer_hard_gates.go) implements the three FR-002 close-time hard gates — executable acceptance (declared **Verification Command:** executed in the worktree, non-zero exit rejects with verbatim output via `close_gate_rejected`, missing command fails open with `verification_command_missing`), quarantine contradiction (`closed-vs-quarantined` while `hydra/quarantine/<id>` exists without an `integrated` label, git errors fail open), and dependency order (chain members hard-reject on non-terminal deps, singletons warn-only). Wired on the completed-close path before the VerifiedClose judge round; rejections reuse `rejectClose` so reopen/attempt-cap/escalation semantics are unchanged. ~90% statement coverage on the new code.
### Fixed
- task-breakdown classifier: explicit `**Type:**` deliverable-shape markers (analysis/audit/test/refactor/spike/deletion) are now parsed into the requirement and override classification in `classifyRequirement` (→ `direct_task`), instead of being visible only to the standalone `routeTaskPattern` API while analysis PRDs compiled to TDD chains; shape-bearing header prefixes (TEST-/REF-/SPIKE-/AUDIT-/DEL-/ANALYSIS-) now also stop description accumulation like other requirement headers.

### Added
- **create-prd skill anti-drift fixes**: mandatory Repo-State Verification phase (verify cited paths/tools/counts/landed-work before drafting, `## Verified Facts` block with proof commands), machine-checkable state claims (3.5b), and an amendment rule (edit normative text in place, no contradicting footnotes). Restored the `doctrine.md` pointer. Motivated by the srv-smart-engine coverage PRD that cited a nonexistent jest.config.ts, a stale spec baseline, and a missing node_modules — none caught until breakdown analysis. Skill synced to the shared Pi config.

### Added
- **PR-wave integration (integration/pr-wave-20261013)**: landed 12 stranded fleet PRs in one gated branch — post-close verification sweep with flake guard + bounded reopen (#285, also heals a test baseline red since #255), evidence-of-use probes with `intel_usage` worker stream events (#281), graphify project-path guard (#286), tool-health degradation drill (#292+#295), hindsight extension-connectivity sensor (#294), dead HindsightLifecycle removal (#293), multi-uid br store friction fix (#296), task-breakdown repo-language detection so Go PRDs stop getting JS-stack acceptance criteria (#299), sandboxed verification runner refactor (#225), Pan wedge-bead signature dedupe with 1h TTL window (#232), escape-rate regression budget + close-time `verificationCommandGuard` (#221). Superseded earlier rounds of the same features (#213–#218, #273–#284, #287) are closed unmerged. Deferred: #230/#231/#234 bead-lifecycle refactors (deep runner.mjs conflicts). Operators: the daemon now re-verifies recently closed beads, dedupes Pan filings across restarts, and emits per-tool usage telemetry.

### Added
- Tool-health degradation drill: `ToolHealthMonitor` + durable file-backed `ToolHealthDedupStore` (internal/core/tool_health.go) — a failing tool probe emits `tool_degraded`, files exactly one fix bead per degradation episode (dedup survives daemon restart), and recovery emits `tool_recovered`; event sink and dedup store exercised un-mocked by the integration drill in tool_health_test.go.

### Added
- Hard-park reconciler: each overseer tick (once per minute) probes projects whose beads carry the `worktree-unavailable` label; after 2 consecutive successful probes the label is cleared, the typed `worktree_unparked` event is emitted, and the bead is schedulable again — no more operator-driven `br label remove` sweeps after a host-level wedge fix. `escalated`/`needs-human` labels stay human-only; probe failures leave labels untouched (fail-open). *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*

### Changed
- **Poisoned-session quarantine threshold lowered 20MB → 8.5MB**, aligned to OpenRouter's gateway body cap (8MB): beyond it, pi's compaction summarize request is itself rejected, making a session permanently unrecoverable — pi's overflow compact-and-retry still rescues sessions below it, so a lower threshold would kill recoverable work. The two-signal rule (size AND zero compaction entries) is unchanged.
### Added
- Tool-health degradation loop (hydra-mt7e, US-INT-01): `ToolHealthMonitor` (`internal/core/tool_health.go`) — probe fails → one `tool_degraded` event + exactly one fix bead (store-level dedup via `tool-degraded-<tool>` signature label, survives monitor restarts); probe recovers → `tool_recovered`. End-to-end drill test runs against a real `br init` workspace and real `EventSink` (no mocks; only the probe is stubbed) — `go test ./internal/core/ -run 'TestToolHealth' -count=1` exits 0.

### Documentation
- Discovery (hydra-mt7e): end-to-end degradation drill audit — `TestToolHealth` and `tool_degraded`/`tool_recovered` events do not exist yet; report maps the GREEN contract (tool-health monitor with injectable probe seam, real EventSink, reuse of fix-sig dedup) at `docs/discovery/tool-degradation-drill-discovery-mt7e.md`.
- Triage `docs/discovery/orphanclaim-churn-triage-hu12.md`: the persistent orphanclaims=6 digest anomaly is a retry-gap snapshot of a gate-failure churn loop (Charon reopened all 9 candidate beads 2–6× on 2026-10-06; loop self-terminates via escalation) — no reconciliation bug, no manual requeue, disk 49% (stale claim).
### Added
- Escape-rate regression budget (NFR-001): a bead's declared verification command (metadata `test_command` / a "Test Command:" description line) is now executed daemon-side before any completed close — verified-close and satisfied-close both reject (reopen) when it exits nonzero, and the singleton-integrate path shares the same Close route, so no close path can reach closed-completed on a red command (fail-open only when no gate runner is attached). A weekly `PostCloseSweeper` (`overseer.postclose_sweep`, default on) re-runs the verification command of beads closed in the last 7 days and flags escapes with the `postclose-escape` label + typed `postclose_escape` event; the weekly query is `scripts/escape-rate-weekly.sh`. *Authored by a Hydra dogfood run.*

### Fixed
- **task-breakdown now detects repo language and stops emitting JS-stack acceptance criteria for Go/Python repos.** `detectRepoLanguage` (go.mod / package.json / requirements.txt / pyproject.toml) drives language-appropriate development constraints (go vet, pytest.approx), lint-gate command + acceptance (go vet ./... / gofmt instead of Biome), SYNTAX/config formatter wording, and suppresses the JS monorepo scaffolding injection on non-JS repos; unknown-language repos keep legacy behavior. Fixes the tool-health PRD class where a Go daemon campaign received unsatisfiable `pnpm`/`toBeCloseTo()`/Biome criteria.
- **Poisoned-session quarantine now requires TWO signals (size >20MB AND zero compaction entries), preventing false positives on legitimately long tasks.** Pi's session file is append-only, so a productive session with many successful compactions eventually crosses 20MB while its live context stays healthy. Audit of 1,232 session files: healthy max 8.5MB with compactions present; the six poisoned sessions were 89-159MB with zero compactions. Read errors fail open (assume healthy).

### Fixed
- **Post-close verification sweep GREEN behavior implemented** (`internal/core/postclose_sweep.go`): `scan()` now re-verifies recently closed beads carrying `metadata["test_command"]` (within 24h) by running the command in the project path; fail→re-run→pass = `postclose-flake` label (no reopen); both runs fail = comment with both outputs + reopen to open + `postclose-reopened` label + typed `postclose_reopened` event; already-reopened bead failing again = `needs-human` (bounded auto-reopen). Store/list errors fail open. Start() wires a 1h ticker loop. Fixes the full-suite gate failing on the pinned RED contract tests.
- **Worker quarantines poisoned pi sessions before resume.** A session JSONL >20MB (~4M tokens, ~3x a 1.3M context window) can never fit the model context again; resuming it guaranteed every turn failed on context-overflow while re-POSTing the full transcript. On 2026-10-08 this produced 132 failing executions of one bead and ~148GB of request bodies with zero completions. Oversize sessions are now moved to `sessions/.poisoned-quarantine/` so the worker starts fresh. Operators: recover quarantined files manually if ever needed.

### Fixed
- **br CLI invocations now run with an isolated per-call `TMPDIR`.** br's fsqlite WAL layer stages files under fixed names in `$TMPDIR` (`beads.db`, `bd-beads.json`, `beads.db-fsqlite-ns-gate`, ...); any two concurrent br processes — even against different stores — collided on those names, producing cross-store WAL phantom writes and `SYNC_CONFLICT` lockouts (observed 2026-10-07: hydra fix beads phantom-duplicated into srv-jobfile's WAL, store unreadable for hours, 15-29 leaked `.br-wal-index-*` dirs per store). The daemon's beads executor now gives every invocation a private `TMPDIR` (created and removed per call). Operators should also sweep stale `/tmp/beads*` staging debris after incidents.

### Changed
- **Post-close sweeper refactor** (`internal/core/postclose_sweep.go`): eligibility folded into `sweepableCommand(bead)`, reopen comment body extracted to `reopenCommentBody`, event types named (`evPostCloseNeedsHuman`/`evPostCloseReopened`); added `BenchmarkPostCloseSweepVerifyBead` (~84 ns/op, 0 allocs). Behavior-preserving; TestPostCloseSweep suite passes.
### Fixed
- Post-close verification sweep GREEN (hydra-5rln): internal/core/postclose_sweep.go scan() now implements the pinned contract — closed beads with a recent `test_command` are re-verified in the project checkout; one failing run is re-run once (pass → `postclose-flake`, no reopen); double failure reopens the bead (StatusOpen + `postclose-reopened` label + comment carrying BOTH failing-run outputs); failure after a prior reopen adds `needs-human` instead of a second reopen; >24h/commandless beads skipped. Real 1h Start/Stop loop replaces the inert RED stub.
### Added
- Evidence-of-use probes (deep tier): workers emit an `intel_usage` stream event (hindsight / cass / graphify_fallback fields) before every agent invocation, and a hourly evidence sweep emits `tool_degraded` (probe:"evidence") when a project has recorded worker activity but Hindsight serves zero memories — the signature of a silently degraded recall stack; zero worker activity emits nothing and probe errors fail open.

- `collectDiskWatermark` units bug fixed — `fs.Bfree` is in blocks but was mixed with a byte-scaled total, reporting a constant ~99% used on every filesystem. This was the true root cause of the recurring disk-99% campaign anomaly fleet-wide (448 `disk_cleanup_run` events on 2026-10-06 alone); the digest now computes used% block-vs-block, matching `df`. Regression test pins digest used% within ±2 of the real statfs block ratio. Operator: rebuild + drain-restart the daemon to stop the false filings.

- **Post-close verification sweeper implemented** (`internal/core/postclose_sweep.go`): `scan()` was an inert RED stub, so the 4 pinned behavior tests (reopen/flake-guard/bounded-reopen/real-git) failed the daemon gate. GREEN: sweep re-verifies recently closed beads with a recorded test command in the project's default-branch checkout (fail→fail re-run → reopen + `postclose-reopened` + comment carrying BOTH outputs incl. exit-status fallback via `runEvidence`; fail→pass → `postclose-flake` label, no reopen; already-reopened → `needs-human`), Start = hourly scan loop, all fail-open.

Fail-open class — extension died at bootstrap, agent "skips silently if the tool is unavailable" — is now surfaced as a loud `[HindsightSensor]` log line plus a `hindsight-extension` degrade-counter entry (visible once cross-process degrade reporting lands); never gates the bead verdict.

### Removed
- **Dead `HindsightLifecycle` service deleted.** `NewHindsightLifecycle` had zero call sites and the overseer field was declared but never set or read; the Hindsight server's lifecycle is owned by systemd. Removing it drops 369 lines of unreached server spawn/monitor code.

### Fixed
- **Post-close verification sweep implemented (was an inert RED stub).** `PostCloseSweeper.scan()` now re-runs each recently closed bead's recorded `test_command`: pass → nothing; fail→pass re-run → `postclose-flake` label, no reopen; two consecutive failures → comment with both failing outputs + errors, reopen to open, `postclose-reopened` label; repeat failure on an already-reopened bead → `needs-human` instead of a second reopen. The pinned suite (`TestPostCloseSweep*`) previously failed every gate run against the inert stub.
### Fixed
- **Post-close verification sweeper implemented (gate fix).** `PostCloseSweeper.scan()` now implements the RED-pinned contract: closed beads <24h old carrying `metadata["test_command"]` get their command re-run in the project checkout; a passing re-run labels `postclose-flake`, a persistently failing command reopens the bead with `postclose-reopened` and a comment carrying BOTH failing-run outputs, and a second failure on an already-reopened bead adds `needs-human` instead of a second reopen. All errors fail open; Start/Stop hourly loop nil-safe. Unblocks the `internal/core` gate (4 failing postclose tests).
- **Daemon now stops managing user-owned bead stores (multi-uid friction, bug hydra-6g4u).** br's fsqlite WAL layer uid-locks its namespace sidecars: a store written by the daemon (uid 999) can never be opened interactively by the repo owner (`fsqlite refused its namespace sidecar ... owned by uid 999`), and every further daemon write deepens the churn. A new fail-closed spawn preflight (`spawnPreflightStoreOwnership`, event `spawn_blocked_store_ownership`) suppresses spawns into any project whose `.beads` directory is owned by a uid other than the daemon's; the probe itself fails open (missing store / unreadable path must not stall the fleet). Auto-resumes if ownership is corrected (chown the store to the daemon uid, or manage the repo user-side). Verified live: hydra's own repo passes, ajantha-owned stores (webapp, srv-smart-engine, uid 2008) are suppressed with the remedy in the error.
- **br CLI invocations now run with an isolated per-call `TMPDIR`.** br's fsqlite WAL layer stages files under fixed names in `$TMPDIR` (`beads.db`, `bd-beads.json`, `beads.db-fsqlite-ns-gate`, ...); any two concurrent br processes — even against different stores — collided on those names, producing cross-store WAL phantom writes and `SYNC_CONFLICT` lockouts (observed 2026-10-07: hydra fix beads phantom-duplicated into srv-jobfile's WAL, store unreadable for hours, 15-29 leaked `.br-wal-index-*` dirs per store). The daemon's beads executor now gives every invocation a private `TMPDIR` (created and removed per call). Operators should also sweep stale `/tmp/beads*` staging debris after incidents.

### Changed
- **Post-close sweeper refactor** (`internal/core/postclose_sweep.go`): eligibility folded into `sweepableCommand(bead)`, reopen comment body extracted to `reopenCommentBody`, event types named (`evPostCloseNeedsHuman`/`evPostCloseReopened`); added `BenchmarkPostCloseSweepVerifyBead` (~84 ns/op, 0 allocs). Behavior-preserving; TestPostCloseSweep suite passes.
